       source-level half: it walks every file under core/src/main/java and fails on the mode
       names themselves, so a mode cannot return through a file the reflection sweep does not
       scan (a new Screen, a menu, a save field). BalanceSchema.java is the one excluded file:
       it DEFINES the banned tokens, and its own members are covered by check 2 itself.
  WHAT WAS REMOVED: four modes (the old RECRUIT/normal/NIGHTMARE/ULTRA tables in GameBalance),
  the Difficulty enum on PlayerStats, and the mode argument at run creation. StatsStore drops
  any legacy mode key on load (best-effort; a legacy record has always loaded fine because
  load() asks only for the keys it knows). There was no difficulty-selection screen to remove —
  World hard-coded the normal tier — so the mobile touch flow into a run is unchanged.
  WHY: the modes were wrong twice. They contradict the design (in this roguelike, difficulty is
  chosen INSIDE the run — route choice and the priced map, region danger, depth via the coupling
  curve, scarcity swings and loot luck — never from a menu), and even as implemented they were
  fake: modes changed ONLY starting stats while every enemy number, scarcity target and telegraph
  cap stayed anchored to the normal tier's 205 eHP, so three of the four were never actually
  balanced. Deleting three makes the contract EXACT for the one real game instead of exact for
  one mode out of four, and the telegraph fairness caps (25% un-telegraphed, 35% boss single hit)
  now provably protect the actual player pool in every run. No value was re-derived by this
  order — it removes an assumption, not numbers.
  TUNING: difficulty moves by moving THE dials — the scarcity band, the heal-drain band, the
  boss survival ratios, the region danger multipliers. One set, one game. If accessibility
  options are ever wanted they must be framed as exactly that (e.g. animation speed) and never
  as balance-touching modes; this rule is the tripwire.

R-LADDER (RuleKind.LADDER) — THE POWER LADDER (balance-overhaul order 1)
  Every floor has a THREAT LEVEL equal to its depth. Weapon damage climbs a compound ladder
  (base * LADDER_GROWTH^(L-1) * rarity * levelGapMultiplier(L - d)); enemy HP and damage climb
  FITTED compound curves (ENEMY_HEALTH_GROWTH / ENEMY_DAMAGE_GROWTH); max HP/armour climb
  PLAYER_VITALITY_GROWTH per character level. A weapon BELOW the floor's level takes
  LEVEL_GAP_PENALTY_BASE^levels (floor LEVEL_GAP_FLOOR, scaled by LEVEL_GAP_STEEPNESS); one
  ABOVE gains LEVEL_GAP_BONUS_PER_LEVEL (capped). All values: BalanceConfig SECTION 20.
  THE ONE EXPECTED PLAYER: GameMath.expectedPlayerAtDepth(d) -> util/ExpectedPlayer — weapon
  level d, the region's expected rarity (mean of the WEAPON_DROP_TIER band ends), character
  level 1 + EXPECTED_LEVELS_PER_DEPTH*(d-1), offence cards as a PP %-lift (50% of the level
  budget), defence cards as flat eHP (50%), vitality-scaled max HP/armour. Its depth-1 DPT is
  REFERENCE_PLAYER_DPT and its depth-1 eHP REFERENCE_PLAYER_EHP (the anchors stay the depth-1
  anchors). The audit, the simulator and the boss derivation read this one model.
  Six sub-checks, for every depth 1..RUN_FINAL_DEPTH (25) and every non-boss role's reference
  archetype (CHAFF Gore Biter, SOLDIER Void Shroud, BRUISER Shell Brute, MINI_ELITE Iron
  Stalker). Turns are CONTINUOUS (eHP / per-hit damage) so one-hit rounding hides nothing:
    L1 ON-CURVE FLAT  on-curve TTK and TTD within +/-15% of their depth-1 values.
    L2 LAG PUNISHED   weapon level d-2 -> TTK >= 1.5x on-curve; d-4 -> >= 2.5x; the start
                      weapon (L1 COMMON) at d >= 5 -> >= 3.0x.
    L3 AHEAD BOUNDED  weapon level d+2 -> TTK >= 0.75x on-curve.
    L4 LEVEL FELT     on floor d, level d vs level d-1: DPT gain >= +25%.
    L5 RARITY FELT    each tier step >= +8% DPT; LEGENDARY >= +40% vs COMMON (equal level,
                      abilities excluded).
    L6 VITALITY FELT  character level d-3 -> TTD <= 0.80x on-curve.
  THE FIT: ENEMY_HEALTH_GROWTH 1.139 is the least-max-deviation compound rate over 1..25
  against the expected player's per-hit growth (ladder x rarity x offence lift); on-curve TTK
  wobbles within +/-13% (the residual is the per-region rarity step). ENEMY_DAMAGE_GROWTH
  1.103 is fitted the same way against vitality 1.09 + flat defence-card eHP (+/-14%).
  With the defaults L4 = 1.08/0.82 = 1.32, L2 = 1.73 at d-2 and 3.01 at d-4, L3 = 0.78,
  L6 = 0.79 at worst. Enemy HP at depth 25 is ~23x depth 1 (fraction-based health bars; AS10).
  WHY: a weapon level used to be +10% linear, enemy HP grew 4.2%/floor, rarity could not raise
  power and max HP never grew — so a lazy and a prepared player differed by a rounding error
  (the owner: "I can play with a level-1 weapon very confidently even on higher levels").
  REPLACES R-DEPTH and R-GEARGATE (override record: balance-overhaul order 1 OVERRIDE CLAUSE;
  retired when the ladder goes live in gameplay, CP2).

R-LADDER-AFFORD (RuleKind.LADDER_AFFORD) — the shop rung is always in reach (balance-overhaul order 1)
  At every depth 1..RUN_FINAL_DEPTH the Fabricator's LEVEL UP rung (priced through
  GameMath.shopPrice at LADDER_LEVEL_UP_POWER_POINTS = 1.5 PP) costs <= LADDER_AFFORD_FRACTION
  (0.5) of one COMBAT floor's modelled credit income (model-floor kill bounties scaled by
  CREDIT_DEPTH_SCALE + expected credit chips). Measured: 54 of 159 credits at depth 1 (0.34),
  184 of 389 at depth 25 (0.47). WHY: keeping a favourite gun on the ladder must be a real
  trade against supplies (about half a floor's credits), never out of reach.

NEVER-SOFTLOCK (GameMath.emergencySupplyTriggers, not a schema band — a floor guard)
  A player who wastes ammo must never die to an empty inventory screen. When total
  remaining potential damage (all reserves * per-shot damage + one melee swing) falls
  below remainingFloorDemand * EMERGENCY_SUPPLY_FRACTION (0.25), the next enemy drop
  is forced to be ammo for an equipped weapon, capped ONCE PER FLOOR (EnemyManager).
  0.25 sits far below the scarcity band, so a hoarder never trips it — it only turns
  "standing empty-handed" deaths into fighting-retreat deaths; melee (fist, no ammo)
  is the true backstop. Proven by a synthetic-inventory unit test (fires when starved,
  never when supplied, honours the once-per-floor cap). RunStats records ammo picked
  up, credits earned/spent, heals used and emergency triggers as order-9 telemetry.

S-LAG (RuleKind.SIM_LAG) + THE LADDER REPORT (balance-overhaul order 1)
  S-LAG: HOARDER-START-WEAPON's median death depth <= SIM_LAG_MAX_MEDIAN_DEPTH (6) — a weapon
  that never climbs the ladder ends the run early. Reported every balanceSim run; ENFORCED since
  balance-overhaul order 7 deleted the navigation waiver (measured 3.0 at CP1).
  THE LADDER REPORT (sim/LadderReport, printed under the bands, OUTPUT not an assertion): the
  ordinary matrix stalled on the first floors when it was built (fixed by order 7), and a probe
  still isolates the ladder from the route, so the report plays one-floor PROBES started at
  every depth 1..25 (SimSettings.ladderProbe, SIM_LADDER_PROBE_SEEDS_PER_DEPTH seeds per kit):
  TACTICAL with the ON-CURVE kit (guns at the floor's level, the region's lowest drop tier) and
  HOARDER-START-WEAPON with the START kit (L1 COMMON), both on the on-curve character (vitality
  + the model's card lifts), so the ratio is the weapon ladder alone. Every hit dealt records
  enemy max HP / hit (EnemyManager.PlayerHitListener, sim-only); every attributed hit taken
  records full pool / hit. Per role and depth band it prints the median of each and lag/on.
  First read-out: played on-curve hits-to-kill stay flat 1..25 (SOLDIER 3.3 -> 3.4, CHAFF 1.9 ->
  2.1); the start kit's ratio climbs 1.7x (1-5) -> ~24x (21-25); A2's SOLDIER depths 3-5 read-
  out is 2.35x (target >= 1.5x). TacticalPolicy buys the rung for its equipped weapon (R15);
  HOARDER-START-WEAPON never does.

S-GATE / S-FAIR / S-SKILL / S-ROUTE / S-ECONOMY / S-SUPPLY / S-SWITCH / S-HEAVY / S-BUILD / S-BOSS / S-NAVIGATION / S-RELIC / S-TWIST / S-SOFTLOCK (+ S-LAG above) (RuleKind.SIM_*, order 9)
  THE BEHAVIOURAL BANDS — the only rules in this document that are checked against PLAY
  rather than against arithmetic. Everything above proves the numbers are self-consistent;
  it cannot prove the game plays the way they claim (knowledge-doc problem 1: "verified by
  harness only — NEVER playtested"). Because the game is strictly turn-based, a whole run is
  SIMULABLE: sim/BalanceSimulator plays SIM_SEED_COUNT (200) runs per policy through the REAL
  systems — real LevelGenerator floors via the real route pipeline, real EncounterBudgetPlanner
  rosters, real EnemyManager turns, the real damage/drop/XP/economy path, and the real
  PlayerController action entry points — with a scripted PlayerPolicy instead of touch input.
  Values live in BalanceConfig SECTION 18; the bands are evaluated in sim/BehavioralBands and
  gated by `./gradlew balanceSim` (BalanceSimTest), NOT by the fast `./gradlew test`.

  THE POLICIES (deliberately simple heuristics, not AI — the gap between them IS the game's
  difficulty range, since the game ships exactly one difficulty):
    NAIVE                 attacks the nearest enemy, never guards, ignores hazards and cardinal
                          lines, heals at <30% vitality, never switches weapons, walks the
                          shortest route, picks route nodes at random.
    TACTICAL              keeps range, breaks the cardinal line vs ranged, READS THE RED FLOOR (a heavy
                          committed at it: stagger it with a close spread blast, else GUARD it when
                          faced, else step off the marked tiles, else guard — balance-overhaul order 4
                          CP7b, see S-HEAVY), manages ammo across weapons, heals early,
                          buys the shop backstop, takes upgrades, routes by the order-7 node EV.
                          Balance-overhaul order 3 (CP7): takes the C4 SWITCH hint whenever it is up
                          (SimWorld computes it exactly as World.updateMatchupHint does — the real
                          MatchupAdvisor + PlayerController jump), and keeps its kit DIVERSE: a found
                          gun of a DamageClass the loadout lacks replaces the redundant / emptiest
                          slot (never the last gun with ammo; PlayerPolicy.slotToReplaceForGroundWeapon).
    HOARDER-START-WEAPON  TACTICAL (it delegates chooseAction, so it INHERITS the heavy answer), but NEVER equips a weapon it finds or buys — the user's
                          boss-cheese reproduction, kept as a permanent regression case.
    TACTICAL-CLOSE / -MARKSMAN / -PYRO-DEMO / -BULWARK  (balance-overhaul order 5, S-BUILD) TACTICAL with a
                          BUILD BIAS (sim/BuildBias): perk picks prefer the bias tags, then synergy, then the
                          most-ranked tag; keystone picks take the bias tag's keystone when offered. The bias
                          does NOT steer weapons (TACTICAL's ground-weapon rule is a damage-class diversity
                          rule; a tag preference there would move S-SWITCH / S-HEAVY). SIM_BUILD_SEED_COUNT
                          (200) seeds each. Plain TACTICAL's perk pick is synergy first, then most-ranked tag.
    TACTICAL-BUILD-SAMPLE plain TACTICAL over SIM_BUILD_SAMPLE_SEED_COUNT (600) seeds — S-BUILD's dominant-tag
                          run set. Sized when only ~8-10% of TACTICAL runs levelled up (order 5: 20 of 200;
                          49 ranked of 600); with navigation fixed (order 7 CP1) 278 of 600 end ranked.
                          Its first 200 seeds replay plain TACTICAL exactly; no other band reads it.

  S-GATE      HOARDER-START-WEAPON clears the FIRST boss on 0% of seeds. The headline
              guarantee: R-BOSS-GATE proves the starting loadout cannot win on paper; this
              proves it in play. WHY zero and not "rarely": the original bug WAS the starting
              shotgun grinding a boss down, and a band with slack invites it back.
  S-FAIR      THE RUN OUTCOME (balance-overhaul order 6, F8 — OVERRIDES "TACTICAL median death depth
              in [SIM_TARGET_DEPTH_MIN 8, MAX 14]", constants deleted): TACTICAL win rate (clears the
              depth-25 boss) in [0.20, 0.40]; TACTICAL reaches depth 10 on >= 0.60 of seeds; TACTICAL
              median death depth over its LOSSES in [11, 18]; NAIVE win rate <= 0.03; NAIVE median death
              depth in [4, 8]; FIRST BOSS: TACTICAL clears it on >= 0.75 of the seeds that reach it,
              NAIVE reaches it on >= 0.40 and clears it on >= 0.15 of all seeds (SECTION 25). All eight
              are depth-derived. The navigation waiver is DELETED (order 7 CP1); the seven lines still
              out of band (all but NAIVE win rate) ride the round-pending waiver (SECTION 4). Unchanged: >= 90% of deaths preceded either by a COMMITTED enemy intent (the
              hit was shown before it landed) or by a sub-25% resource state (the player walked in
              weak). WHY: a death must feel like "I made a mistake", never a dice roll; and a finite
              run must be WON by a prepared player sometimes, never always.
  S-SKILL     TACTICAL's median depth exceeds NAIVE's by >= SIM_SKILL_DEPTH_GAP_MIN (5; OVERRIDE,
              balance-overhaul order 6: was 3, re-set against the 25-floor run). WHY:
              one fixed difficulty (order 8) only works if SKILL decides depth; if this gap
              collapses the game stopped rewarding play and became a wall.
  S-ROUTE     The played per-floor net HP drain sits inside the order-7 modelled trajectory
              band, widened by SIM_ROUTE_TRAJECTORY_TOLERANCE (play dodges fights the ledger
              charges for). WHY: the priced map must survive contact with play.
  S-ECONOMY   RE-BASED by balance-overhaul order 2 (CP7): the mean share of each EXITED floor's
              PLANNED ammo units the player actually picked up sits in [SIM_ECONOMY_SUPPLY_SHARE_
              MIN 0.75, MAX 1.10] (the old "experienced S vs modelled S" compared the player's
              CARRIED ammo against the retired model-floor S), and the never-softlock lifeline
              fires on < 5% of floors. WHY: below the band the planner puts supply where play does
              not reach it; above it, unplanned income is leaking in.
  S-SUPPLY    (balance-overhaul order 2) TACTICAL's mean health fraction leaving a COMBAT floor by
              its exit sits in [SIM_SUPPLY_EXIT_HEALTH_MIN 0.55, MAX 0.90 — RE-BASED from 0.80 by
              balance-overhaul order 4, see below], and ZERO played floors
              hold less reachable heal value than their S4 heal floor. WHY (AS2): "usually full
              HP" was the complaint — a normal floor should cost something; REST and CACHE are
              where HP comes back. NOT waived: it reads exited floors only (and since order 7 almost
              every played floor is exited).
              OVERRIDE (balance-overhaul order 4 CP7b, hub decision): MAX 0.80 -> 0.90. Old rule: a
              competent player leaves a COMBAT floor at <= 0.80 HP. New rule: <= 0.90. Why: TACTICAL now
              answers ~0.99 of heavies (S-HEAVY), which removed the half-pool hits that were the floor's
              drain (exit 0.73 -> 0.85). The prescribed heal re-fit was tried and is exhausted
              (SUPPLY_AVOIDANCE_FACTOR 0.80/0.90 moved it only to 0.83; COMBAT heal is floor-bound at the
              S4 medkit, and lowering S4 breaks the one-medkit guarantee). The scripted reader is a
              perfect reader; real players will land some heavies. "Usually full HP" (AS2) is still
              guarded by the band, at a ceiling that reads a near-perfect reader honestly. Re-check in
              the order-7 feel pass alongside HEAVY_DAMAGE_SCALE.
  S-SWITCH    (balance-overhaul order 3, A8, RE-STATED by idea decision D3 under the override
              clause) — THE C4 HINT WORKS. Three lines, none waived (fight-level, not depth-derived):
              (1) at least SIM_SWITCH_MIN_HINTED_COMBAT_FLOORS (10) TACTICAL COMBAT floors across the
              matrix show a hint, so (2) and (3) are never read off an empty sample; (2) TACTICAL's
              hint TAKE RATE (hint-driven switches / hint episodes) >= SIM_SWITCH_MIN_TAKE_RATE (0.8);
              (3) hint-driven switches per COMBAT floor THAT SHOWED A HINT >= 1.0. A hint-driven
              switch is a SWITCH tap taken while the hint was up that equipped the hinted gun.
              REPLACED RULE: A8 as first written — ">= 1.0 matchup switches per played COMBAT floor".
              WHY REPLACED: measured 0.13 with a take rate of 1.00; only 21 of 167 COMBAT floors ever
              showed a hint, because the start kit (Chaingun + Shotgun, two usable slots) can only
              answer CHITIN and ~85% of sim runs stall on floor 1. That count measures the start
              kit's class spread, not whether the hint works; changing the start kit would reopen
              the order-1/2 fitted bands. The overall per-COMBAT-floor count is still printed (info
              column of line 3 and the MATCHUP REPORT) so a later kit change can be read against it.
  S-HEAVY     (balance-overhaul order 4, CP7b, A8) — A PLAYER WHO READS THE RED FLOOR ANSWERS THE
              HEAVIES THROWN AT IT. Three lines, none waived (fight-level, not depth-derived):
              (1) TACTICAL had at least SIM_HEAVY_MIN_SAMPLE (30) heavies committed AT it (the player's
              tile was in the committed heavy's danger set), so (2) cannot pass vacuously; (2) TACTICAL's
              ANSWERED share = (avoided + guarded + staggered + killed first) / committed-at-player
              >= SIM_HEAVY_TACTICAL_MIN_SUCCESS (0.80); (3) NAIVE, which ignores telegraphs, answers at
              most SIM_HEAVY_NAIVE_MAX_SUCCESS (0.30) — by chance alone. Each committed-at-player heavy
              resolves to EXACTLY ONE outcome (enemy/HeavyOutcome, fired through the observation-only
              HeavyOutcomeListener that EnemyManager exposes; nothing in enemy/ imports sim/):
                AVOIDED       resolved, the player was not hit (left the tiles)
                GUARDED       the hit landed in the guarding player's FRONT arc (riposte)
                STAGGERED     cancelled before resolving — stagger, riposte stun, R6 status stun
                KILLED_FIRST  the source died before resolving (a self-destruct that detonates is
                              resolved first, so it is never counted as killed-first)
                LANDED        unguarded, or a side/back hit — the only failure
              A self-destruct that re-telegraphs its countdown every turn is ONE instance; a heavy
              committed while the player was NOT on a danger tile is not counted (it was not committed
              at the player). The killing-blow-was-a-heavy flag is "a LANDED/GUARDED heavy resolved and
              the player was dead afterwards" (SimWorld, no Player damage tagging needed).
              REPLACED RULE (OVERRIDE CLAUSE): TacticalPolicy's old guard trigger — "GUARD when the
              committed telegraphed damage is >= SIM_TACTICAL_GUARD_DAMAGE_FRACTION (0.20) of what is
              left, never twice running" — is DELETED (the constant too). It guarded on the SIZE of
              any telegraphed hit, facing or not, and it let heavies land. TACTICAL's new answer, in
              order, whenever its own tile is a heavy danger tile (SimView.heavyThreatOnMyTile):
              (1) FIRE a close spread blast (Shotgun / Double-Barrel, source in the facing line inside
              SHOTGUN_STAGGER_MAX_TILES) — a staggered heavy is cancelled (H5); (2) GUARD a heavy it
              already FACES (AS4: the riposte stuns it and leaves it VULNERABLE, and the marine is
              lined up for the free shots; a marine that sidestepped every heavy it faced never shot
              back and looped to the turn cap — measured with the step-first order: 184/200 stalled, S-SWITCH
              red (9 hinted floors, take rate 0.52), S-SUPPLY 0.90; hub-accepted order below); (3) else STRAFE/step to a
              walkable, unoccupied, non-danger neighbour (prefers a tile off every ranged lane, then
              one on a cardinal line with the source); (4) else GUARD anyway (cornered) — a primed
              self-destruct with a turn to spare is turned toward first, or shot if already faced.
              NAIVE is unchanged and ignores telegraphs. HOARDER inherits TACTICAL's answer.
  S-BUILD     (balance-overhaul order 5, A6) — BUILDS DIFFER IN PLAY. Three lines:
              (1) DEPTH SPREAD: the four build-biased variants' median death depths satisfy max / min <=
              1 + SIM_BUILD_DEPTH_SPREAD (1.25) — no bias is a trap or a must-pick. Depth-derived: ENFORCED
              since order 7 CP1 (navigation fixed; measured 1.00, every variant median 3.0).
              (2) SAMPLE (ENFORCED, never waived): the TACTICAL-BUILD-SAMPLE row has >= SIM_BUILD_MIN_SAMPLE
              (30) runs that ended holding a perk rank, so (3) is never read off an empty sample.
              (3) DOMINANT TAG: over those runs, no single BuildTag is the end-of-run dominant tag
              (PlayerBuild.highestRankTag; ties keep the earliest tag, counted in the report) on more than
              SIM_BUILD_MAX_DOMINANT_TAG_SHARE (0.40) of them. WAIVED by owner ruling D7 (2026-10-11), moved to
              the order-7 round-pending waiver at CP1 (still 0.47, MARKSMAN 130 / CLOSE_QUARTERS 111 of 278 ranked
              runs — a round-1 J2 question). Order-5 measurement: 0.47 (MARKSMAN 23 / CLOSE_QUARTERS 17 / ENERGY 6 /
              PYRO 1 / DEMOLITION 1 / VITALITY 1 of 49 ranked runs, 600 seeds) because ~91% of sim runs never
              level up and every ranked run holds exactly ONE rank, so "dominant tag" is just the first
              synergy pick off the Chaingun (MARKSMAN) + Shotgun (CLOSE_QUARTERS) start kit. Enforced
              when a playtest round meets it.
              HONEST CAVEAT (order 5; order 7 CP1: ~46% of runs now end ranked, still no keystone taken): only ~8-10% of runs level up at all, NO
              keystone is ever offered in the sim (no run reaches level 5), and the four biased variants
              play identical depths (median 2.0, ~175/200 stalled, mean level 1.10) — S-BUILD measures the
              first perk pick and nothing deeper. R-PERK / R-KEYSTONE / R-CARRY carry the build claim on
              paper meanwhile. SEEDING FIX (CP6): PerkDeck and EnemyManager's build stream (Breacher /
              Salvager rolls) now finalise their seed through GameMath.splitMix64 — java.util.Random's first
              draw is correlated across adjacent seeds, so the sim's sequential run seeds drew the SAME
              first synergy perk (LANE_DISCIPLINE) on every run (and one run's floors drew the same first
              build roll). Before the fix the share read 0.80 (MARKSMAN 16 of 20).
              Asserted by BalanceSimTest.buildBiasesStayCloseAndNoTagDominates (exactly the depth spread and
              the dominant-tag share may be waived).
  BUILD REPORT (sim/SimReport, OUTPUT not an assertion; balance-overhaul order 5, CP6): per TACTICAL row (plain,
              the 600-seed sample, the four biases) — runs, median depth, stalled, mean character level,
              keystones per run, runs with no perk rank, tie-decided dominant tags; the dominant-tag
              distribution per row; then per Perk and per Keystone offered / picked / pick rate on level-up
              screens for the sample row and for the four biased variants together (shop-bought ranks are
              not offers).
  HEAVY REPORT (sim/SimReport, OUTPUT not an assertion; balance-overhaul order 4, CP7b): per policy and
              depth band (1-5, 6-10, 11-15, 16+; SIM_HEAVY_REPORT_BAND_LAST_DEPTHS) — heavies committed at
              the player, the five outcomes, the answered share, statuses the armour shield held off
              (H9), nominal hazard damage dealt to ENEMIES per floor (barrel blasts + source-less fire /
              toxic DoT ticks), and runs whose killing blow was a heavy; then the heavy KINDS committed at
              each policy and an "unresolved at floor end" count (a heavy still pending when the floor
              ended — it is in the committed count but has no outcome).
  MATCHUP REPORT (sim/SimReport, OUTPUT not an assertion; balance-overhaul order 3): per policy and
              depth band — EFFECTIVE / RESISTED hits per floor (EnemyManager's onEnemyMatchupHit seam),
              hint-driven switches per floor, each DamageClass's share of landed hit damage (the
              class the activation armed; burn ticks excluded), plus the S-SWITCH alternatives line:
              switches per COMBAT floor, hint take rate, switches per COMBAT floor that showed a hint.
  S-BOSS      (NEW, balance-overhaul order 6 CP7, F10; RuleKind.SIM_BOSS) for every BossTable row, over the
              TACTICAL seeds that REACH it: clear rate in [SIM_BOSS_CLEAR_RATE_MIN 0.50, MAX 0.90] (the first boss
              >= SIM_FIRST_BOSS_TACTICAL_CLEAR_MIN 0.75), fight-turns coefficient of variation (first exchange ->
              kill) <= SIM_BOSS_MAX_FIGHT_TURNS_COV 0.30, median health fraction at the kill in [0.30, 0.60];
              CoV / health need >= SIM_BOSS_MIN_SAMPLE 10 kills. Fifteen lines, all depth-derived, riding the
              order-7 round-pending waiver (CP1, navigation fixed: 54 seeds reach depth 5, 0 clear). WHY: "bosses are sometimes OK,
              sometimes too hard, sometimes too easy" (owner) — consistency is a played property.
  S-NAVIGATION (NEW, balance-overhaul order 7 CP1, P8; RuleKind.SIM_NAVIGATION) — one line per policy in the
              matrix: at most SIM_MAX_STALLED_FLOOR_FRACTION (0.10) of the floors it PLAYED may run the turn cap
              out. NEVER waived: every depth-derived band stands on it. It replaced the order-9 navigation waiver.
              Measured at CP1: TACTICAL 0.00 (0 of 200 runs), HOARDER 0.00, NAIVE 0.03 (10 runs — its chase).
              WHY a band, not a test detail: the navigation waiver let the sim report "median death floor 2"
              for two whole series while measuring its own pathing. The fixes (all in sim/, never game rules):
              no facing a DIAGONAL threat (it can never be faced — the largest single stall); a burst gun below
              one burst with no reserve counts as DRY; never turn toward a threat the gun cannot answer; plan
              THROUGH a sleeping blocker and shoot it (SimNavigator's enemy-blind fallback); a floor with no
              stairs is a boss floor — hunt the boss; re-scan the boss floor's exit after the kill; and the
              LoopBreaker (SIM_LOOP_*: <= 4 tiles in 16 turns with nothing fired / reloaded / healed opens a
              12-turn PUSH that skips the sidesteps and routes through enemies).
  S-RELIC     (NEW, relics-run-changing-artifacts CP8; RuleKind.SIM_RELIC) — NO RELIC IS A TRAP AND NONE IS MANDATORY.
              Five TACTICAL PROBE ROWS (BalanceSimulator.relicProbePolicies(), SIM_RELIC_PROBE_SEED_COUNT 120 seeds
              each; ids TACTICAL-NO-RELIC, TACTICAL-RELIC-COMMON, TACTICAL-RELIC-RARE, TACTICAL-RELIC-LEGENDARY,
              TACTICAL-RELIC-CURSED): each LEAVES every relic offer and is instead GRANTED nothing (the baseline) or ONE
              relic of its rarity at depth-1 arrival, round-robin over that rarity's NON-AFFINE pool by run index, so each
              relic gets floor/ceil(120 / pool) runs. Bands read MEAN ending depth (medians are integers near 3):
              SR1 each COMMON / RARE / LEGENDARY row's mean depth / NO-RELIC in [1 - SIM_RELIC_ROW_TRAP_TOLERANCE 0.10,
              1 + SIM_RELIC_ROW_MAX_LIFT 0.40]; SR2 the CURSED row in [1 - SIM_RELIC_CURSED_ROW_TOLERANCE 0.15, 1.40];
              SR3 rarity is felt — LEGENDARY mean depth >= COMMON; SR4 every non-affine relic with >=
              SIM_RELIC_MIN_RUNS_PER_RELIC (10) probe runs in [1 - SIM_RELIC_PER_RELIC_TRAP_TOLERANCE 0.25, 1 +
              SIM_RELIC_PER_RELIC_MAX_LIFT 0.60] (30 lines); SR5 every non-affine relic got >= 10 probe runs (always
              enforced); SR6 standard TACTICAL holds >= 1.0 relic at run end on average (the acquisition wiring is live).
              ENFORCED, never waived. Tag-affine relics are banded by the audit only (a probe start weapon may not carry
              their tag, which would make them look like traps). Measured at CP8: NO-RELIC mean depth 3.15; SR1 1.02 / 1.02
              / 1.03; SR2 1.04; SR3 1.01; SR4 0.86 .. 1.12; SR5 minimum 13 runs; SR6 1.01. HONEST CAVEAT: TACTICAL dies near
              floor 3, so deep-paying relics under-read and the audit (R-RELIC*) is the tighter gate; SR6 is THIN — TACTICAL
              took the start relic on 200 of 200 runs, so it proves the RUN_START wiring, not the BOSS / MYSTERY paths; the
              quarantine_locker is not played at all (SimWorld applies no EVENT choices). Asserted by
              BalanceSimTest.noRelicIsATrapAndNoneIsMandatory. RELIC REPORT (sim/SimReport, OUTPUT not an assertion):
              per probe row mean / median depth and lift; per relic runs, mean depth and ratio; TACTICAL offers seen /
              taken / left by source; MODEL vs PLAYED kill share and doors per floor against RELIC_VALUATION_KILL_SHARE /
              _DOORS_PER_FLOOR. RoundSummary row R1 (relic rows mean-depth lift LEGENDARY / NO-RELIC, IN/OUT) and the
              LadderReport column "relics (expected)" ride the same change. See docs/relic-system.txt.
  S-TWIST     (NEW, floor-twists CP8; RuleKind.SIM_TWIST) — NO FLOOR TWIST IS A DEATH TRAP. Read from the TACTICAL
              row's played COMBAT floors (FloorLedger.twistId / diedOnFloor): ST1 for each twist with >=
              TWIST_SIM_MIN_FLOORS (60) floors, its floor-death rate / plain (untwisted) COMBAT's over the SAME depth
              range <= TWIST_SIM_MAX_DEATH_RATE_RATIO (1.50); an under-sampled twist reports "n/a (sample n)" and does
              not fail. ST2 every twist was dealt at least once (the roll is live). ENFORCED, never waived. Asserted by
              BalanceSimTest.noFloorTwistIsADeathTrap. Measured at CP8 (200 TACTICAL runs): blackout 13 floors, salvage
              12, hunted 1, plain 201 — all three ST1 lines n/a; the hunter never arrived. HONEST CAVEAT: TACTICAL dies
              near floor 3, so the band is close to vacuous today; the audit (R-TWIST) is the real gate. TWIST REPORT
              (sim/SimReport, OUTPUT): per twist + plain — floors, floor-death rate, depth range, mean turns, mean HP
              lost, hunter arrivals / kills. Policies stay twist-BLIND (AS23): the sim plays what the map deals.
  S-SOFTLOCK  Zero seeds end unable to damage anything. Structurally guaranteed rather than
              tuned: the Fist is wired at run start and never removed, so "no ranged ammo" is a
              fighting retreat, not a dead end. The band exists to keep that structural
              guarantee from being quietly removed.

  DETERMINISM is part of the contract: every stream is seeded from the run seed (level
  generation, route map, weapon roller, perk deck (seed finalised through GameMath.splitMix64
  since balance-overhaul order 5 CP6), and — since order 9 — the enemy
  spawn/drop/effect rolls, weapon accuracy rolls, hazard rolls and the AGILITY dodge roll,
  all of which used to be unseeded). Same seed + same policy => byte-identical ledger;
  BalanceSimTest asserts it. Unseeded randomness in a gameplay path is now a determinism bug.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
SECTION 4 — WAIVER POLICY + LIVE WAIVERS
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
A waiver is the ONLY way a value may sit outside its band without failing the
build. Register it in BalanceSchema's static block via
  waive(kind, subject, reason, expiryCondition)
and mirror it HERE in the same commit. A waiver must state a real reason and a
condition under which it expires; BalanceReport prints all active waivers so
they can never fade into folklore. Never weaken a band to make a test pass.

G5 LIMIT (balance-overhaul order 8): an ADMISSION failure — a MISSING declaration, pricing row or dangling
reference (SECTION 5) — can NEVER be waived; BalanceSchema.waive() throws for RuleKind.ADMISSION. Only a
VALUE outside its band is waivable, and only here.

LIVE WAIVERS (the order-7 round-pending bands + the bespoke-node pricing below):
  (DELETED by balance-overhaul order 3, AS8: [WEAPON_POWER] Railgun (full charge)
   45.0 vs heavy 24-32 — "gated by slug scarcity". R-WEAPON is retired; the Railgun
   is governed by R-ROLE, its PLATED niche and R-ROLE-5's ammo check. R-ROLE has no
   waiver and BalanceAuditTest pins that.)

  (DELETED by balance-overhaul order 7, CP1 — the order-9 NAVIGATION waiver. It covered every
   depth-derived S-band because the scripted policies ran the floor's turn cap out on ~85% of runs.
   Order 7 fixed the navigation (diagonal threats, unusable burst guns, sleeping blockers, boss floors,
   the stale boss-floor exit, the LoopBreaker push) and replaced the waiver with the ENFORCED band
   S-NAVIGATION: every policy leaves <= SIM_MAX_STALLED_FLOOR_FRACTION (0.10) of its played floors
   stalled — measured TACTICAL 0.00, NAIVE 0.03. S-LAG, S-BUILD's depth spread, S-ROUTE and S-FAIR's
   readable deaths are in band and ENFORCED with no waiver.)

  ROUND-PENDING (balance-overhaul order 7, CP1; owner decision 2026-10-14) —
  BalanceSchema.waiveRoundPendingBands:
  [SIM_FAIR] TACTICAL win rate / reaches depth 10 / median death depth (losses) / first-boss clears (of
  seeds reaching it), NAIVE median death depth / reaches the first boss / first-boss clears;
  [SIM_SKILL] TACTICAL vs NAIVE depth gap; [SIM_ECONOMY] experienced vs planned supply, emergency lifeline
  floors; [SIM_SHOP] TACTICAL offers bought per Fabricator visit; [SIM_BUILD] TACTICAL dominant-tag share;
  [SIM_BOSS] all fifteen lines.
    reason:  with navigation fixed the simulator measures the GAME, and the game is out of band
             (CP1 matrix: TACTICAL median death floor 3, 54 of 200 reach the first boss and 0
             clear it, win rate 0, dominant tag 0.47). Bringing it into band is what the order's
             playtest rounds are for — owner PLAY NOTES + the sim, at most ~5 master knobs a round
             (P5/P6) — not a blind sim-only retune before the owner has played. NAIVE win rate
             (0.00 <= 0.03) is in band and NOT waived.
    expires: balance-overhaul order 7 CP6. Each line is deleted in the round its band is met; any
             line still out after round 3 goes, with its evidence, into the follow-up idea
             request (P9).

  [CALM_COST] rest EV discount (balance-overhaul order 2, CP6; re-pointed by order 6)
    reason:  the route ledger is now priced on each floor's NodeSupplySpec plan. The old
             ledger credited the bespoke MED-BAY room with 35% of a combat floor's ordinary
             loot it never placed; priced honestly REST sits well below COMBAT's EV (0.58 at
             d25 against the [0.10, 0.40] band). Order 6 left REST alone (SCOPE OUT: owner
             "REST is ok"). The EVENT half of this waiver is RETIRED by order 6 CP6: EVENT
             contents were re-expressed (F15) and EVENT is governed by R-EVENT-VALUE.
    expires: with the balance-overhaul order 7 feel pass (REST contents).

  [BUILD_CARRY] R-CARRY SCAVENGER (balance-overhaul order 5, owner ruling D3)
    reason:  SCAVENGER's value is supply, shop and carrier economy, not reference-fight TTK / TTD;
             R-CARRY's conversion under-reads it (1.09 vs the combat tags' ~1.75-2.08). Still printed.
    expires: when order 6 re-derives the economy with SCAVENGER in the expected player.

  [PERK] PK1 WILDFIRE r2 felt d5 / d15, PK1 WILDFIRE r3 felt d5 / d15, PK1 WILDFIRE breakpoint by r3
  (D4 resource: SCENARIO_AMMO S5 Incinerator) (balance-overhaul order 5, owner ruling D6)
    reason:  every reference group fight ends inside the first spray or two, so burn duration is
             invisible to the scenario math. Rank-1 FELT and PK2 stay enforced.
    expires: a long-group reference scenario exists (order 7 feel pass).

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
SECTION 5 — ADD CONTENT — THE CONTENT BALANCE CONTRACT (balance-overhaul order 8)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
REPLACES the new-game-balancr order-1 "NEW-CONTENT CHECKLIST" (override record: the old list told a
contributor what to remember; this contract makes the build refuse what they forgot). It is the ONE
place that says how new balance-bearing content enters the game. Every content-creating agent, doc and
idea file points here; nothing restates it.

HOW IT IS ENFORCED
  RuleKind.ADMISSION in util/BalanceSchema holds eighteen C-rules, one per content kind (K1-K18 below).
  Each iterates the game's OWN collection — EnemyType.values(), the room registry after its headless
  bootstrap, RegionPlan.defaultPlan(), every public number in BalanceConfig — so new content is found
  without anybody listing it. util/BalanceAuditTest runs one test per C-rule under `./gradlew test`.
  A C-rule checks that an item is DECLARED, PRICED and REACHED BY ITS BAND RULE; the band verdict itself
  stays with the band rule (R-ENEMY, R-ROLE, R-HEAL, ...), which keeps its waiver policy.
  BalanceReport prints the ADMISSION table (every item, per kind, admitted or what is missing).

READING A C-RULE FAILURE
    ADMISSION C-WEAPON: FLECHETTE_CARBINE — no declared niche for SPREAD (ROLE_DECLARED_NICHE_BY_CLASS) ...
  = rule C-WEAPON (kind K5), content id FLECHETTE_CARBINE, then EVERY missing item, "; "-joined, each
  naming what is missing and where it goes. Fix the declaration; re-run. An ADMISSION failure is never
  a band question, so there is nothing to tune and nothing to waive.

GENERAL
  G1  "Balance-bearing content" = anything that changes damage, HP, supply, threat, XP, credits, prices,
      run shape or player power. Every such addition is exactly one kind K1-K18; if none fits, it is K18
      and the idea that adds it extends this contract (a new K row + its C-rule) in the same idea.
  G2  Every checklist has six parts: DECLARE (numbers and flags that must exist), BAND (the rule/range it
      must land in), PRICE (how its cost/value is computed — never hand-set where a formula exists),
      REGISTER (the only way in), COMMUNICATE (how the player learns it), DONE (the checkable state).
  G3  Every number lives in BalanceConfig, every formula in GameMath, every row is a register() or enum
      row — never a switch.
  G4  Adding balance-bearing content is FULL LANE (idea file + checkpoints). The short lane stays open
      only for content that is supply-, spawn- and power-neutral — a SUPPLY_NEUTRAL room blueprint, a
      sprite, a bark line — and C-ROOM checks the neutrality.
  G5  An ADMISSION failure (a MISSING declaration, pricing row or dangling reference) can NEVER be waived:
      BalanceSchema.waive() throws for RuleKind.ADMISSION. A VALUE outside its band is the band rule's
      business and may be waived only through SECTION 4 (reason + expiry, mirrored there).

THE CHECKLISTS (copy the block for each item an idea adds into its BALANCE ADMISSION section)
  K1  ENEMY ARCHETYPE (EnemyType row)
      DECLARE  role(), family() (trait inherited, override explicit), base HP,
               base damage, cadence, range, positional multiplier, move-set with
               every SpecialAbility, HEAVY flags, min spawn depth / region, the
               EncounterGroupTemplate slots it may fill, tacticalVerb().
      BAND     TP in its role band; the order-1 R8 TTK/TTD role targets at
               depth 1 (R-LADDER L1 across 1..25); trait-aware eHP (order 3);
               heavy verbs in the R-HEAVY band and avoidable.
      PRICE    TP via EnemyType.baseThreatPoints (cycle-averaged, heavy
               avoidance discount); XP derived from TP (never hand-set).
      REGISTER EnemyType row; spawn symbol — a new FIXED spawn symbol follows the
               CLAUDE.md STRICT RULE in one commit; at least one group template
               slot; family voice (audio) and bestiary lines come structurally.
      COMMUNICATE trait glyph (from family/trait), intent icons for its verbs,
               danger markers for heavies, ORA archetype line (BestiaryCatalog
               string id resolves).
      DONE     C-ENEMY green; it appears in BalanceReport ENEMIES/LADDER tables
               and spawns in the sim FLOOR REPORT at its depths.
  K2  ENEMY FAMILY / TRAIT (EnemyFamily, EnemyTrait)
      DECLARE  trait; trait glyph id; a MatchupCatalog row for EVERY DamageClass;
               family voice binding (audio); family bestiary lines.
      BAND     R-ROLE-1..3 still hold with the new trait's scenario added (a new
               trait adds one reference scenario to R-ROLE).
      DONE     C-FAMILY green; matchup table complete (no missing cell).
  K3  ENEMY SPECIAL VERB (SpecialAbility)
      DECLARE  pricing equivalence (special-verb pricing row), HEAVY flag stated
               explicitly (true/false), danger-tile shape if HEAVY, telegraph
               (commits >= 1 turn ahead if > 25% eHP), ORA ability line id.
      BAND     R-TELEGRAPH; R-HEAVY if heavy; SUMMON verbs under the hard caps.
      DONE     C-VERB green; TELEGRAPH AUDIT table lists it.
  K4  BOSS (boss EnemyType + BossTable row)
      DECLARE  BossTable row (depth, archetype, target fight turns, survival
               ratio); phase count; verb fractions; heavy verbs flagged.
      BAND     R-BOSS-* (gate, fair, verb cap incl. the 50% heavy cap, reward,
               ammo); S-BOSS consistency.
      PRICE    derived by BossBalance from ExpectedPlayer — never literal HP.
      DONE     C-BOSS green; every BossTable depth resolves to a declared boss.
  K5  WEAPON (Weapon subclass + ItemType)
      DECLARE  DamageClass, role/niche scenario, ammo type, base damage / clip /
               range / falloff / reload at LV1 COMMON, ladder participation
               (only the Fist is exempt), special properties (pierce, splash,
               knockback, stagger).
      BAND     R-ROLE-1..5: it must be best in >= 1 reference scenario OR add a
               new scenario (then every existing class is re-checked); it may not
               take the ONLY niche of an existing class; no class best in > 2.
               R-LADDER (it follows the ladder).
      PRICE    scenario turns-to-clear; ammo feasibility; shop price via
               GameMath.shopPrice from its PP.
      REGISTER ItemType + weapon classification (C-WEAPON), WeaponRoller tier
               rules, ground sprite and HUD per docs/weapon-creation-guide.txt.
      COMMUNICATE class glyph + colour, STRONG VS / WEAK VS on the compare card,
               switch-hint eligibility (MatchupAdvisor sees it automatically).
      DONE     C-WEAPON green; SCENARIO table row; sim MATCHUP REPORT shows it
               when carried.
  K6  WEAPON ABILITY (WeaponAbility)
      DECLARE  magnitude per level, cap, BuildTag, melee/ranged eligibility,
               MAJOR flag, signature flag.
      BAND     priced PP >= ABILITY_MIN_FELT_PP at its expected level; fits the
               tier budgets (R-ABILITY).
      PRICE    GameMath.abilityPowerPoints.
      DONE     C-ABILITY green; roll sweep never exceeds a tier ceiling.
  K7  PERK / KEYSTONE (Perk, Keystone)
      DECLARE  BuildTag, ranks, per-rank modifiers, attribute grant; keystone
               gain AND cost modifiers.
      BAND     R-PERK (felt, focused), R-KEYSTONE (>= 30% niche gain, >= 10%
               cost elsewhere), R-CARRY re-checked for every tag.
      DONE     C-PERK green; PERKS/BUILDS tables list it; name/effect ids resolve.
  K8  ITEM / PICKUP / CONSUMABLE (ItemType; heal, armour, utility)
      DECLARE  category; effect as a FRACTION of max (HP/armour) or an explicit
               non-HP utility effect; stack cap; cleanse behaviour; supply
               category (which SupplyPlan category it counts toward) or the
               explicit declaration NOT_FLOOR_SUPPLIED; pickup symbol (existing,
               or STRICT RULE).
      BAND     R-HEAL (survival-turns bought) for HP/armour items; R-SUPPLY
               (counted in the plan).
      PRICE    supply value in PP; shop price via GameMath.shopPrice.
      COMMUNICATE inventory icon + name/description ids; HUD feedback on use.
      DONE     C-ITEM green; placed only by SupplyPlanner (or declared shop/
               reward-only).
  K9  AMMO TYPE (AmmoType)
      DECLARE  generosity factor, reserve cap, box size, shop box price source.
      BAND     R-ROLE-5 (feasible), reserve banking target, R-SUPPLY.
      DONE     C-AMMO green.
  K10 STATUS EFFECT
      DECLARE  ONE definition (R-DOT); magnitude as a fraction of max HP (DoT) or
               a multiplier; duration; stacking; armour-shield interaction
               (blocked or not, cost); cleanse (which item); HUD icon id.
      BAND     R-DOT; its appliers' TP includes it (special-verb pricing).
      DONE     C-STATUS green; status row shows it with turns remaining.
  K11 HAZARD / GAMEPLAY PROP (explosive barrel kinds, fire / toxic tiles, any
      prop or tile that deals damage or applies a status)
      DECLARE  damage as a FRACTION (of the floor's reference enemy HP for
               enemies, of max HP for the player); two-sidedness (hits player,
               enemies, or both); the status it applies (K10); DamageClass for
               matchups (EXPLOSIVE / FIRE / ...); lifetime and spread; placement
               rule (which planner places it, near which groups).
      BAND     hazard TP via GameMath.hazardTileThreatPoints inside the floor
               budget; un-telegraphed player damage <= 25% (R-TELEGRAPH).
      REGISTER existing symbols only, or the STRICT RULE; placement only through
               the encounter/supply planners (never hand-placed by a generator).
      COMMUNICATE a readable sprite/tile and, if it can hit the player, a visible
               warning before it does (fuse, glow, danger marker).
      DONE     C-HAZARD green; BalanceReport HAZARDS table lists it.
  K12 ROOM BLUEPRINT (RoomBlueprint)
      DECLARE  its supply stance: SUPPLY_NEUTRAL (emits NO pickup and NO enemy
               symbols — only slots) or SLOT_PROVIDER (tagged supply / group
               slots the planners may use).
      BAND     building it in isolation emits no pickup or enemy-spawn symbol.
      DONE     C-ROOM green. (A neutral blueprint may stay short-lane, G4.)
  K13 LEVEL GENERATOR (GeneratorId)
      DECLARE  SupplySlotProvider implementation; footprint band per region;
               the node types it serves.
      BAND     R-SUPPLY and R-DENSITY sweeps pass for every node type it serves.
      DONE     C-GENERATOR green; sim FLOOR REPORT row per node type.
  K14 ROUTE NODE TYPE (RouteNodeType + profile)
      DECLARE  NodeSupplySpec row; NodeLevelProfile; route-card threat/reward
               lines (C1 of order 2); region weights.
      PRICE    NodeEconomics derived from the spec (never hand-set); DangerTier
               derived (R-PIPS-DERIVED).
      DONE     C-NODE green; R-ROUTE-* green.
  K15 ROUTE EVENT / MYSTERY OUTCOME / NODE AFFIX
      DECLARE  every reward and cost in ladder currencies (weapon LV, perk rank,
               HP/armour fraction, credits in floors of income, weapon drop);
               an affix that raises threat declares its matching reward.
      BAND     R-MYSTERY-EV; event value rule (order 6 F15); R-RISK-PREMIUM.
      DONE     C-EVENT green.
  K16 SHOP OFFER TYPE
      DECLARE  its value in PP; category weight; availability rules.
      PRICE    GameMath.shopPrice only — a literal credit price fails.
      BAND     R-CREDITS v2 still holds.
      DONE     C-SHOP green.
  K17 REGION (RegionSpec)
      DECLARE  REGION_TP_BUDGET_MULTIPLIER entry, elite weight, BossTable row for
               its last floor, ambience + crest rows, story-region mapping.
      BAND     R-REGION; run-outcome bands.
      DONE     C-REGION green.
  K18 ANY NEW BALANCE NUMBER (the catch-all)
      DECLARE  every new public numeric field in BalanceConfig is either the
               SUBJECT of at least one schema rule, or listed in
               BalanceSchema's UNBANDED table with a one-line reason.
      DONE     C-CONSTANT green.

WHAT EACH C-RULE CHECKS (the build's half of each checklist; BalanceSchema.admission<Kind>Results())
  C-ENEMY      every EnemyType: role / family / trait; positive HP, damage, range, cadence, move rate; a POSITIONAL_MULT_*
               multiplier; tactical verb; every move-set verb priced; a wind-up heavy evaluated by R-HEAVY; legal
               min spawn depth; ORA archetype line resolves. Non-boss also: TP + hit bands for its role, finite TP,
               XP == round(XP_PER_THREAT_POINT x TP), a unique spawn symbol, an EncounterGroupTemplate slot.
  C-FAMILY     every EnemyFamily: trait, ALERT / ATTACK / DEATH family voices (WIND_UP is shared by design), early +
               deep bestiary lines. Every EnemyTrait: a positive matchup cell for every DamageClass, a glyph (unless
               FLESH), a WeaponRoleModel reference scenario whose archetype presents it.
  C-VERB       every SpecialAbility: a SpecialVerbPricing row; a damaging verb is a HeavyAttack committed >= 1 turn
               ahead and evaluated by R-HEAVY; a summon verb has positive summon caps; ORA ability line resolves.
  C-BOSS       every BossTable row: legal depth in BOSS_TABLE_DEPTHS, positive dials, BossVerbSpec rows, BossStats
               derive, R-BOSS-GATE / R-BOSS-FAIR reach it. Every BOSS EnemyType is fought on some row.
  C-WEAPON     every WEAPON ItemType is RANGED / MELEE / AMMO_FALLBACK. RANGED: a WeaponRoleModel row (class + ammo),
               a declared niche (ROLE_DECLARED_NICHE_BY_CLASS naming a real scenario, or BALLISTIC = the generalist),
               R-ROLE reaches its class, positive hit / clip / range, in SupplyPlanner.weaponDropPool(). MELEE: a
               BLADE / BLUNT class (register its instance in BalanceSchema meleeWeaponInstances()).
  C-ABILITY    every WeaponAbility: family / trigger / rank / BuildTag; finite PP >= 0 at every weapon level; a
               SIGNATURE is eligible for at least one weapon family (signatures are legendary-slot rolls).
  C-PERK       every Perk: tag, ranks, >= 1 effect, name + line, reached by PK1. Every Keystone: tag, >= 1 gain AND
               >= 1 cost, both lines, reached by R-KEYSTONE. Every BuildTag: >= 1 perk, exactly one keystone, an
               R-CARRY row.
  C-ITEM       every non-weapon ItemType: a declared supply stance (ITEM_SUPPLY_STANCES — a SupplyCategory or
               NOT_FLOOR_SUPPLIED + reason); a consumable is a priced heal (HealPickupSpec) or declared utility;
               a stackable has a cap; a description.
  C-AMMO       every AmmoType: a scarcity row, positive generosity / reserve cap / box / banking target, an AMMO
               pickup ItemType, a positive shop box price source.
  C-STATUS     every StatusType: a STATUS_DECLARATIONS row (shape DOT_FRACTION / DOT_FLAT / MULTIPLIER / CONTROL,
               magnitude + duration constants that resolve and are > 0, a DoT fraction in (0, 1), armour-shield
               interaction, a cleanse item that is a consumable).
  C-HAZARD     every HAZARD_DECLARATIONS row (barrel 'E', barrel 'g', fire 'i', toxic 'q', the Sump Wretch's death
               splash 'q'): fraction constants resolve
               (player side in (0, 1]), the symbol is a real hazard cell, its status is declared, a DamageClass (or a
               stated reason), a placement owner that exists. BalanceReport HAZARDS lists the registry.
  C-ROOM       every RoomBlueprint: supplyStance(); a SUPPLY_NEUTRAL room stamped in isolation over 120 seeds places
               no pickup and no enemy-spawn symbol.
  C-GENERATOR  every GeneratorId: registered, builds headless, serves >= 1 node type, R-SUPPLY sweep results for each
               (direct SupplySlotProvider generators and the FloorPopulator / PocketSupplyRegions ones alike).
  C-NODE       every RouteNodeType: NodeTypeDefinition, level profile, NodeSupplySpec, NodeEconomics row, a whole
               route-card promise (threat word + reward line, both or neither), a region multiplier everywhere.
  C-EVENT      every FacilityEvent choice, MysteryOutcome and affix / twist: priced; every effect in ladder
               currencies (fractions, floors of income, weapon levels); a delegate profile that exists; an affix
               that raises threat (budgetScale > 1) declares a reward.
  C-SHOP       every OfferCategory: a SHOP_OFFER_SPECS row (PP value, weight constant), shopPrice > 0 at every
               depth, and the LITERAL-PRICE TRIPWIRE: real rolled offers (d1/5/15/25 x 12 seeds, all five
               categories) must cost exactly GameMath.shopPrice of their declared value.
  C-REGION     every RegionSpec: a REGION_TP_BUDGET_MULTIPLIER entry, an ELITE multiplier, a BossTable row on its
               last floor, ambience + crest, a StoryRegion.
  C-CONSTANT   every public numeric BalanceConfig field is in CONSTANT_SUBJECTS (the RuleKind that bands it), UNBANDED
               (with a reason) or GRANDFATHERED (the pre-order-8 fields — SHRINK-ONLY); a stale name fails too.
               The build checks that a CONSTANT_SUBJECTS entry exists and names a kind; that the named rule
               really reads and bands the field is the reviewer's check.

EDGE CASES
  - Content that fits two kinds (a turret that is an enemy AND a hazard) satisfies BOTH checklists.
  - Hand-made .txt levels are exempt from K12/K13's supply rules (they are authored), but every symbol
    and every content item they use is still admitted.
  - Removing content: the same C-rules catch the dangling reference (a template slot, a BossTable row,
    a niche scenario, a shop offer or an UNBANDED / GRANDFATHERED name pointing at nothing).
  - A registry that can only be bootstrapped with LibGDX is not allowed for balance-bearing content: the
    audit must see it headless. Split it (headless data / render half) as tileset and audio do.
  - An idea file written before this contract: its implementer fills the BALANCE ADMISSION block at CP0.
  - RELICS are not a K row: a relic is 1-3 PerkEffect rows priced by R-RELIC / R-RELIC-CURSE /
    R-RELIC-STACK / R-RELIC-SOURCE (its rarity x valuation must resolve a band — the line COVERAGE used
    to carry). Follow docs/relic-system.txt section 9 (ADD A RELIC); a new PerkModifier kind is K7 work.
  - The player start is not content: the ONE PLAYER_START_* block is tuned in place (R-SINGLE-DIFFICULTY).

THE WORKED EXAMPLES (hypothetical; numbers illustrative — real ones must pass both gates)
  X1  ENEMY — "Scrap Hound" (K1)
      DECLARE  role CHAFF; family MACHINE (trait SHIELDED, inherited); base HP 30,
               damage 10, cadence 1, melee, positional 1.15 (fast melee);
               move-set: none; HEAVY: none; min depth 6 (region B); template
               slots: every CHAFF slot (RABBLE / SKIRMISH / FIRETEAM / ESCORT pairs,
               BROOD, CALM PAIR); behaviour RUSHER (EncounterBehaviour, from its data).
      BAND     TP ~22 inside the CHAFF band; depth-1 targets met for CHAFF
               (1-2 on-curve hits vs FLESH-equivalent; SHIELDED makes the
               Assault Rifle take 2-3, an ENERGY weapon 1).
      PRICE    TP from baseThreatPoints; XP derived.
      REGISTER EnemyType row; needs a spawn symbol -> STRICT RULE commit (check
               docs/tile-symbols.txt for a free one); joins the chaff slots by its role.
      COMMUNICATE SHIELDED hexagon on its bar; MACHINE family voice; ORA line
               story.bestiary.type.scrap_hound (no numbers).
      DONE     C-ENEMY green; ENEMIES + LADDER tables list it; sim FLOOR REPORT
               shows it in region B+ packs.
  X2  WEAPON — "Flechette Carbine" (K5)
      DECLARE  DamageClass SPREAD; niche: new scenario S9 "CHITIN group of 2 at
               3-4 tiles"; ammo SHELLS; LV1 COMMON: 18 per flechette volley,
               clip 6, range 5, falloff 0.10, reload 1; no knockback, stagger at
               <= 2 tiles only.
      BAND     best in S9 by >= 20% over the best BALLISTIC; does NOT beat the
               Shotgun in S6 (the Shotgun keeps its niche); best in <= 2
               scenarios; S1-S8 re-checked with S9 added.
      PRICE    SCENARIO table row; shell feasibility; shop price from PP.
      REGISTER ItemType + classification; WeaponRoller tiers; sprite/HUD per the
               weapon guide.
      COMMUNICATE SPREAD fan glyph; compare card "STRONG VS: chitin".
      DONE     C-WEAPON green; R-ROLE green with S9; MATCHUP REPORT shows use.
  X3  ITEM — "Trauma Kit" (K8)
      DECLARE  category MEDICAL; restores 0.30 of max HP over 3 turns (0.10 per
               turn, interrupted by nothing); cleanses BLEED; stack cap 2; supply
               category NOT_FLOOR_SUPPLIED — sold in shops only, so it needs no
               pickup symbol. (Had the designer wanted it on the floor, it would
               declare supply category HEAL and either reuse an existing pickup
               symbol or add one through the STRICT RULE.)
      BAND     R-HEAL survival-turns within band for its fraction.
      PRICE    supply value in PP -> GameMath.shopPrice.
      COMMUNICATE inventory icon + ids; heal-over-time shown on the status row.
      DONE     C-ITEM green; shop table lists its price; no generator places it
               (the supply planner skips NOT_FLOOR_SUPPLIED items).

THE CONTRIBUTOR'S LOOP
  1. The designer's idea file fills one K block per content item in its BALANCE ADMISSION section, and
     DECIDES each band (never "the builder will tune it").
  2. The implementer adds the content through its registry and declares every field. A forgotten
     hardened declaration does not compile (A3: EnemyType, Weapon, WeaponAbility, Perk, Keystone,
     RoomBlueprint); a forgotten pricing row, band miss or unresolved id fails `./gradlew test`.
  3. Both gates (`./gradlew test`, `./gradlew balanceSim`); regenerate SECTION 6 from BalanceReport.
  4. reviewer checks the BALANCE ADMISSION block against these checklists; any unmet DONE is a FAIL.

BLOCK & DEFEND (order-3)
role          baseBlk   blkMax   decay  survTurns in?   
------------------------------------------------------------------
SOLDIER            15       60       1       0.60 OK    
BRUISER            20       60       1       0.80 OK    
MINI_ELITE         27       60       1       1.08 OK    
survTurns = baseBlk / REFERENCE_PLAYER_DPT (turns of survival one DEFEND buys).
in? = OK when survTurns is in [0.6, 2.0] — ~1 turn bought, never a stall.
BRUISER also braces on a fixed cadence every 3 turns; CHAFF/BOSS never brace.
Turtle trigger: HP <= 50% and the player is positioned to hit next turn.

TACTICAL MODIFIERS (order-6)
power            mult    cap effect                                  
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
VULNERABLE      x2.00      2  target takes +dmg; TTK 4 -> 2 turns
WEAK            x0.65 1hit/refresh  attacker deals -35% (softens a telegraphed hit)
EXPOSED             -  1 hit  next hit ignores Block; lasts 2 turn(s), 1 hit
BACKSTAB        x1.30 conditional  from behind facing; TTK 4 -> 3 turns
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Reference SOLDIER eHP = 85, player DPT = 25. VULNERABLE is stack-capped so even
at max stacks it shaves ~1 turn off a soldier, never trivializing a boss's eHP.
BACKSTAB is priced as a conditional (like crit): it needs positioning, so its
reliable value is self-limiting and it stays a nudge, not an execution check.

LEGEND: in?/gr?/<0.6?/band = OK inside band, UNDER below, OVER above.
  sustDPT = (clip*dmg)/(clip+reload).  SCENARIO: turns to clear (R-ROLE), * best, ! ammo-infeasible.
  TP = (atkDmg/cadence) * (eHP/refDPT) * posMult.  TTD/TTK = golden ratio.
  S = SUPPLY/DEMAND (ranged ammo vs sum of enemy eHP).  perWpnS = that weapon's share.
  bankFloors = full reserve / floor demand (anti-hoard target ~1.5).  netHpDrain = INCOMING - HEAL_SUPPLY.
Out-of-band rows FAIL BalanceAuditTest unless waived — see docs/game-balance-authority.txt.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
SECTION 7 — THE CHANGE PROTOCOL (new-game-balancr order 9) — MANDATORY
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
This section SUPERSEDES the workflow in docs/game-balance-knowledge.txt SECTION 21
(that section now points here). It is the whole series' closing move: values in one
place, rules enforced, and PROOF that the game plays the way the math says.

EVERY change to util/BalanceConfig.java ships with all four of these:

  1. GREEN AUDIT      `./gradlew test`
     Fast (seconds), runs on every commit. BalanceAuditTest evaluates every rule in
     BalanceSchema and fails the build on any unwaived violation. This is the
     gatekeeper for the NUMBERS.

  2. GREEN SIM        `./gradlew balanceSim`
     Slow (whole simulated runs), run on balance changes and nightly in CI.
     BalanceSimTest plays SIM_SEED_COUNT runs per policy through the real systems and
     checks the behavioural bands (S-GATE ... S-BUILD). This is the gatekeeper for the BEHAVIOUR.
     Its plain-text summary lands in core/build/reports/balance-sim/summary.txt —
     read it even when it is green: "median death depth moved 9 -> 6" is a sentence
     you can act on long before a band breaks.

  3. UPDATED LIVING TABLES
     Re-run BalanceReport and paste its output over SECTION 6. The tables are a VIEW
     of the schema; a stale view is worse than none, because it reads as current.

  4. ONE CHANGELOG LINE (below)
     Which dial moved, and WHY. One sentence. The reason is the part that cannot be
     recovered from the diff six months later.

WHEN THE SIM AND THE MODEL DISAGREE, the sim wins — but only after you check WHICH
is wrong. A band can fail because the game got worse, or because the harness got
better (a policy that now finishes floors it used to stall on will move every depth
statistic). The report's per-policy columns (median depth, stalled, readable,
bossClear, softlock, level gap) are there to tell those two apart.

THE TUNING LOOP the whole series was built to enable:
     telemetry says it FEELS wrong (the RUN AUTOPSY on the death screen, and the
     same block logged at run end)
  -> adjust the BAND or the TARGET in BalanceConfig, never the call site
  -> the derived dials re-derive themselves
  -> `./gradlew test` confirms the model is still self-consistent
  -> `./gradlew balanceSim` confirms it still PLAYS that way
  -> humans confirm the feel.
  Numbers chase feel; the schema keeps them honest.

CHANGELOG
  balance-overhaul order 8 — THE CONTENT BALANCE CONTRACT. New RuleKind.ADMISSION with eighteen C-rules (C-ENEMY ...
    C-CONSTANT, one per content kind K1-K18) and one BalanceAuditTest method each; RuleKind.COVERAGE RETIRED (folded
    into C-WEAPON / C-ITEM / C-AMMO / C-ENEMY / C-VERB; its relic line moved to R-RELIC-SOURCE). SECTION 5's
    NEW-CONTENT CHECKLIST REPLACED by ADD CONTENT (G1-G5, K1-K18, X1-X3). Waiver policy gains G5 (ADMISSION is never
    waivable). Declaration hardening (E3): EnemyType.heavyWindUp() / moveSet() abstract, RoomBlueprint.supplyStance()
    (new level/RoomSupplyStance), requireNonNull on WeaponAbility / Perk / Keystone tags. C-CONSTANT grandfathers the

    COMBAT's in [ELITE_THREAT_RATIO_MIN 1.40, MAX 1.80] per generator x depth, and every ELITE
    floor's reward weapon at level >= d+1 in or past the anchor room (the placer seats the reward
    weapon before the vault ammo; when the anchor group fills its room the weapon lies on the
    anchor's own tile). Route card C1 lines: NodeTypeDefinition.supplyPromise(threat, reward).

  balance-overhaul order 2 / CP1-CP3 — FULL FLOORS, FAIR SUPPLY (the supply half). Added
    BalanceConfig SECTION 21 (SUPPLY & DENSITY), route/NodeSupplySpec (+ registry, one row per
    node type), level/SupplyPlanner + FloorPopulator + SupplySlotSurvey, and R-SUPPLY (planner
    level + the generator sweep). Every generator now builds through the planner; carriers
    replace per-kill drop rolls (both never-softlock lifelines kept). RETIRED (override clause):
    R-SCARCITY, R-SCARCITY-DEPTH, R-HEALDRAIN-DEPTH -> R-SUPPLY. DELETED constants:
    ENEMY_AMMO_DROP_CHANCE, MELEE_KILL_AMMO_DROP_CHANCE, LEVEL_GEN_AMMO_CHANCE_PER_ROOM, the

    loot chance (server / large / armory / command / hazard rooms, LEVEL_GEN_RANDOM_ROOM_WEAPON_
    CHANCE), CREDIT_CHIPS_PER_FLOOR_MIN/MAX (+ chip jitters), MODEL_FLOOR_ROOM_COUNT /
    _AMMO_TYPE_COUNT, LevelGenConfig.medkits/armourKits/*ChancePerRoom and RouteMapConstants.

    legacy box model (ROUTE_MODEL_LEGACY_{ROOM,KILL}_AMMO_BOXES 1.6 / 1.2).

  balance-overhaul order 1 / CP5 — the shop LEVEL UP rung (R13): every Fabricator stocks a
    guaranteed LEVEL UP offer for the EQUIPPED weapon (outside the weighted roll; ShopContext now
    carries the equipped weapon) while its level < depth + 1; no shop level-up ever raises a weapon
    above depth + 1 (offer filter + purchase guard); the Fist never gets a rung. Every weapon
    level-up costs BalanceSchema.ladderLevelUpPrice(depth) (LADDER_LEVEL_UP_POWER_POINTS 1.5 PP);
    SHOP_WEAPON_LEVEL_UP_POWER_POINTS (10 PP) is deleted. R-LADDER-AFFORD green.

  balance-overhaul order 1 / CP4 — BossBalance re-derived from GameMath.expectedPlayerAtDepth
    (HP from its DPT, DPT/verb caps from its eHP, the gate from the start weapon on the ladder,
    reward in depth-1 ammo terms at the depth's price level); S-LAG band (waived with the
    navigation bands); the LADDER REPORT probe matrix; TacticalPolicy buys the rung (R15);
    PolicySummary's level gap reads the model's expected character level. All R-BOSS-* green.

  balance-overhaul order 1 / CP2+CP3 — the ladder is LIVE. Weapon hits compose through
    GameMath.weaponLadderDamage (the old Weapon.damageAtDistance used the RAW base damage, so a
    weapon level NEVER reached a hit — the root of the owner's complaint); MAX_WEAPON_LEVEL 27;
    level scaling of accuracy/reload/clip/range/abilities re-spanned 1..27; enemy HP/damage grow
    1.139 / 1.097 per floor; every archetype rebased to R8; max HP/armour grow 1.09 per character
    level; heals are fractions of max. RETIRED: R-DEPTH, R-GEARGATE. RE-STATED: R-ENEMY (R8 hit
    bands + derived TP bands), R-HEAL (fraction bands). RE-FIT at 1..25: R-SCARCITY-DEPTH,
    R-HEALDRAIN-DEPTH, R-XP-PACE (growth 1.2495), R-CREDITS, R-CARD-BREAKPOINT; R-TRAJECTORY upper
    ends; route model reads ammo/credits/heals in depth-1 terms. Ammo boxes/caps ~0.7x; model heal
    counts re-synced to the generator. balanceSim: every enforced band still green (same waivers).

  balance-overhaul order 1 / CP1 — added BalanceConfig SECTION 20 (THE POWER LADDER), the
    GameMath ladder methods, util/ExpectedPlayer, R-LADDER + R-LADDER-AFFORD (asserted on the
    formulas) and BalanceReport's LADDER table. Nothing wired into gameplay yet (CP2-CP6).
    Fitted growth: enemy HP 1.139, enemy damage 1.103, vitality 1.09 (starting points were
    1.09 / 1.07 / 1.07 — the fit also carries rarity and card lift, so the rates rose).

  encounter density (this commit) — the floor budget now converts into BODIES instead
    of stranding a quarter of itself: the anchor gained a real ceiling
    (ENCOUNTER_ANCHOR_BUDGET_CEILING_FRACTION, replacing a fallback that let one
    bruiser eat 76% of a small floor) and a REMAINDER PASS spends the leftover, so a
    CALM/CACHE floor went from EXACTLY 1 enemy on a ~1,100-tile dungeon to ~5.5 on the
    SAME Threat-Point budget. EVENT_BUDGET_SCALE / GATE_BUDGET_SCALE dropped 0.15/0.10
    -> 0 because they were DEAD (their generators emit no spawns and the ledger already
    priced them at 0), and EnemyBudgetOverride.empty() makes zero expressible at last —
    a non-positive scale used to be read as "unset" and silently became the FULL budget.
    WHY: floors read as abandoned; the budget was authorised but never delivered.
    NOTE: the change is TP-neutral but not eHP-neutral (cheap bodies carry more eHP per
    Threat Point than one bruiser), so SIM_ECONOMY emergency-lifeline floors moved
    0.04 -> 0.06, just past its 0.05 band and inside the existing waiver. Watch it.

  order 9 — added BalanceConfig SECTION 18 (simulation harness
    parameters + the six behavioural bands) and seeded every gameplay RNG stream off
    the run seed. No existing balance VALUE was changed: order 9 is proof
    infrastructure, not a tuning pass. Six bands ship WAIVED (SECTION 4) because the
    scripted navigation, not the balance, is what they currently measure.
